## Fix Before Calling Pipeline Green

- `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`: Deploy workflow path conflicts with infra deploy script default path. Action: Unify /opt/bracc vs /opt/coacc.
- `.github/workflows/security.yml`: Security workflow is useful, but current repo fails two of its gates. Action: Fix compliance docs and public privacy query violations.
- `README.md`: Primary repo documentation, but compliance gate says it lacks the expected Legal & Ethics section. Action: Add/update legal section or change the gate.
- `api/Dockerfile`: API image omits repo config files needed by signal registry at runtime. Action: Copy/mount config or make path configurable.
- `api/src/coacc/queries/public_pattern_interadministrative_channel_stacking.cypher`: Public query currently violates public privacy gate by matching :Person. Action: Remove personal labels from public query or reclassify route/query.
- `api/src/coacc/queries/public_pattern_sensitive_public_official_supplier_overlap.cypher`: Public query currently violates public privacy gate by matching :Person. Action: Remove personal labels from public query or reclassify route/query.
- `api/src/coacc/queries/public_pattern_shared_officer_supplier_network.cypher`: Public query currently violates public privacy gate by matching :Person. Action: Remove personal labels from public query or reclassify route/query.
- `api/src/coacc/routers/meta.py`: Active API code/test file, but currently participates in ruff/mypy failures. Action: Fix lint/types before treating API CI as green.
- `api/src/coacc/services/case_service.py`: Active API code/test file, but currently participates in ruff/mypy failures. Action: Fix lint/types before treating API CI as green.
- `api/src/coacc/services/dependency_registry.py`: Active source/signal registry code but currently depends on semantic names that do not match lake source IDs. Action: Introduce catalog alias mapping.
- `api/src/coacc/services/lakehouse_query.py`: Active source/signal registry code but currently depends on semantic names that do not match lake source IDs. Action: Introduce catalog alias mapping.
- `api/src/coacc/services/signal_materializer.py`: Active API code/test file, but currently participates in ruff/mypy failures. Action: Fix lint/types before treating API CI as green.
- `api/src/coacc/services/signal_registry.py`: Active service; runtime depends on config/signal_registry.yml being present. Action: Fix container packaging/config path.
- `api/tests/unit/test_meta_alerts.py`: Active API code/test file, but currently participates in ruff/mypy failures. Action: Fix lint/types before treating API CI as green.
- `api/tests/unit/test_validation_cases.py`: Active API code/test file, but currently participates in ruff/mypy failures. Action: Fix lint/types before treating API CI as green.
- `config/signal_registry.yml`: Active source/signal registry code but currently depends on semantic names that do not match lake source IDs. Action: Introduce catalog alias mapping.
- `config/signal_source_deps.yml`: Active source/signal registry code but currently depends on semantic names that do not match lake source IDs. Action: Introduce catalog alias mapping.
- `infra/scripts/deploy.sh`: Deploy script default path conflicts with GitHub workflow. Action: Unify deploy directory.
- `scripts/check_compliance_pack.py`: Security gate is useful but currently fails because expected legal-index/README section are absent. Action: Add required docs or update gate expectations.
- `scripts/check_public_privacy.py`: Useful public privacy gate; currently catches three public queries with :Person. Action: Fix queries or policy.
- `scripts/lake_reality.py`: Lake reality gate is still hardcoded and exits green on dirty reports. Action: Rewrite as catalog-driven failing gate.
